A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [76] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在2–8B部署相关规模上评测四个VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专用模型BioCLIP（300M）的物种识别能力，发现所有模型在野外相机陷阱图像上均大幅退化，退化源于域缺口而非细粒度判别失败；BioCLIP凭训练数据优势显著领先，但自身域缺口与最佳VLM无统计差异；开放集提示下各模型均产生虚构物种名，其相对排名在两个评测集上完全复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常部署于算力受限、无网络连接的边缘硬件，因此实际需要评估的是可本地部署的小型视觉语言模型（2–8B），而非前沿大模型。本文旨在检验该部署相关规模区间的VLM是否具备真正的分类学知识，并与领域专用小模型对比。

Method: 在96物种任务上，用两个独立采样的评测集，对比四个2–8B VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专用BioCLIP（300M）。图像来源包括干净的iNaturalist照片与6个LILA.science采集的相机陷阱影像，并扩展至200张图像的样本对全部模型进行测试，同时在开放集提示下统计虚构物种名的比例。

Result: 所有模型识别率远超随机水平，但在野外影像上均显著退化，域缺口为9.6–26.6个百分点，且在不同分类层级与两个评测集上一致。BioCLIP在扩展的200张样本上比每个VLM高出33.2–59.2个百分点，尽管其参数规模远小；但BioCLIP自身域缺口（18.0点）与最佳VLM（22.3点）在统计上不可区分。开放集提示下，5.9–9.6%的回复是句法合法但分类学上不存在的物种名，其跨模型相对虚构率排名在两个评测集上完全复现。

Conclusion: 退化主因是图像可辨识度的整体域偏移，而非通用模型缺乏细粒度判别力；BioCLIP的优势主要来自专用训练数据而非模型规模；干净到野外的退化是图像质量变化的固有属性。跨模型虚构率的相对排名比任何单点估计更稳健，可作为可靠结论。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


### [77] [Enabling Knowledge Graph Understanding at Scale with the EXplore Your Graphs ENgine (EXYGEN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11569)
*Harshdeep Singh,Yurui Zhu,Giovanni Colavizza,Matteo Romanello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EXYGEN框架，用RAG+结构化元数据实现KG对话式访问；无微调下SciQA精确匹配0.419，并用谓词覆盖感知并行采样将大规模KG元数据生成提速80倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向规模化知识图谱的对话式访问需求，探索两个关键问题：（1）在仅有自动派生的结构化元数据与小规模图样本、且不做任务特定微调的条件下，LLM的text-to-SPARQL生成效果如何；（2）当KG元数据生成在大规模图上计算不可行时，如何生成该方法所依赖的结构化元数据。

Method: 方法分两部分。其一，将VoID描述与ShEx模式集成进检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，在SciQA基准上对KG派生上下文做消融，最佳配置融合ShEx模式、检索三元组与示例问答-查询对。其二，提出谓词覆盖感知的并行图采样策略，在保持结构多样性的同时保证计算可行性，用于从超大规模KG生成结构化元数据。

Result: 无任何LLM微调下最佳配置在执行结果上达到0.419精确匹配；发现F1等词汇指标难以预测查询正确性，且上下文充分时更大的通用LLM可超越更小的代码专用模型。采样策略在OpenCitations Meta与GESIS上保持高谓词覆盖、三元组损失极小，运行时间降低80倍以上；在ORKG上采样不仅是更快，更是获得完整元数据的唯一可行路径。

Conclusion: 结构化模式上下文与轻量提示可显著降低对微调的依赖，支撑可扩展的KG对话式访问；但缩小与完全微调方法的差距，可能需减少对人工策划问答样例的依赖（如合成生成或基于执行反馈的方法），并需在单一基准之外进一步验证。

Abstract: We present EXYGEN (EXplore Your Graphs ENgine), a framework for knowledge graph (KG) understanding that enables conversational access to KGs at scale. We address two questions in sequence. First, how effectively can LLMs perform text-to-SPARQL generation given only automatically derived structured metadata and small graph samples, rather than task-specific fine-tuning? We integrate VoID descriptions and ShEx schemas into a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline and ablate KG-derived context on the SciQA benchmark. Our best configuration -- combining ShEx schemas, retrieved triples, and example question-query pairs -- reaches an exact match of 0.419 on execution results without any LLM fine-tuning. We further find that lexical metrics such as F1 poorly predict query correctness, and that larger general-purpose LLMs can outperform smaller code-specialized ones once given sufficient context. Second, we ask how to generate the structured metadata that this method relies on from very large KGs, where KG metadata generation becomes computationally intractable. We introduce a predicate-coverage-aware parallel graph sampling strategy that preserves structural diversity while remaining computationally tractable. On OpenCitations Meta and GESIS, it retains high predicate coverage with minimal triple loss and reduces runtime by over 80x; on ORKG, sampling is not just faster but the only tractable path to obtain complete metadata. Together, these results show that structured schema context and lightweight prompting can substantially reduce reliance on fine-tuning for scalable conversational access to KGs, though closing the remaining gap to fully fine-tuned approaches will likely require reducing dependence on curated question-query exemplars -- whether through synthetic generation or an execution-feedback-driven approach -- and validating these findings beyond a single benchmark.

</details>


### [78] [Distributed Optimization of Modular Production Systems using Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Inverse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11615)
*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的强化学习框架，通过引入近似逆过程模型解耦驱动动力学与状态空间动力学，使训练仅在任务空间进行，并在模块化制造实验平台上验证了性能与训练速度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度灵活、模块化的制造系统需要数据驱动的自学习控制方法；传统强化学习在模块化制造单元中面临驱动动力学与状态空间动力学耦合、训练效率低的问题。

Method: 提出一种新的基于模型的强化学习框架，在策略训练中引入近似逆过程模型，将驱动动力学学习与状态空间动力学解耦，从而实现仅在任务空间内的强化学习训练；设计轻量级前馈近似逆模型架构，并将其集成到标准强化学习算法的策略网络中。

Result: 在包含异构生产模块的实验室模块化生产测试平台上进行应用与验证，结果表明该方法在模块化制造单元的性能和训练速度方面均带来效率提升，尤其对离策略（off-policy）算法效果显著。

Conclusion: 近似逆过程模型与策略网络的结合可有效提升模块化制造系统自学习控制的效率，为模块化制造单元的数据驱动控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [When Passing Tests Hides Vulnerabilities: An Empirical Study of Silent Failures in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10548)
*Wenji Bai,Muhammad Waseem,Zeeshan Rasheed,Jaakko Peltonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对基于LLM的智能体代码修复中的"静默失败"（通过语法和功能验证但仍保留或引入安全漏洞的补丁）进行了系统性实证研究。基于7个智能体框架、GPT-4o-mini在两个安全数据集上的1,030条有效执行轨迹，经三轮定性编码与人工验证确认170例静默失败，划分为遗漏、引入、不足三大类及十种细分失败编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究与实践多关注补丁是否通过语法与功能验证，却忽视了那些通过验证但仍保留或引入安全漏洞的"静默失败"，缺乏对其的系统性识别与分类，导致评估体系无法覆盖安全性隐患。

Method: 采用实证研究法，收集7个智能体框架配合GPT-4o-mini在SecurityEval和CVEfixes两个安全聚焦数据集上产生的1,030条有效执行轨迹；通过三轮定性编码和人工验证，最终确认170例静默失败并进行分类归纳。

Result: （i）识别出遗漏（48.2%）、引入（30.6%）、不足（21.2%）三大类静默失败；（ii）归纳出十种细分失败编码，刻画智能体如何遗漏必需安全控制、施加不完整防御或在修复中引入新漏洞；（iii）当前的测试通过式评估和LLM评审角色均不足以暴露或拦截这些失败；（iv）不同框架出现相似的不安全解法，提示存在模型、提示或任务层面的共同影响因素，且单智能体与多智能体系统呈现不同的失败特征。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于研究者和实践者改进LLM智能体代码修复的评估方法，并开发超越功能正确性、覆盖所有生成产物的针对性验证手段，以识别和防范静默安全失败。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for automated code repair have received significant attention in recent years from both research and software engineering practice perspectives. However, limited attention has been paid to patches that pass syntactic and functional verification but still retain or introduce security vulnerabilities. The aim of this research is to systematically identify and categorize such silent failures in LLM-based agentic code repair.
  We conducted an empirical study using 1,030 valid execution traces produced by seven agent frameworks with GPT-4o-mini across two security-focused datasets, SecurityEval and CVEfixes. Through three iterations of qualitative coding and manual verification, 170 confirmed silent failures were identified. The key results are: (i) Three main categories of silent failures were identified: Omission, Introduction, and Inadequacy. Omission accounts for 48.2% of the confirmed failures, Introduction for 30.6%, and Inadequacy for 21.2%. (ii) Ten fine-grained failure codes were classified under these three categories, showing how agents omit required security controls, apply incomplete defenses, or introduce new vulnerabilities during repair. (iii) Current test-passing evaluation and LLM-based reviewer roles were insufficient to expose or intercept these failures in the confirmed cases. (iv) Similar insecure solutions appeared across different frameworks, suggesting possible shared model-, prompt-, or task-level influences, while single-agent and multi-agent systems showed different failure profiles.
  The results of this study will assist researchers and practitioners in improving the evaluation of LLM-based agentic code repair and developing targeted verification methods that go beyond functional correctness and cover all generated artifacts.

</details>


### [80] [Optimizing AI Inference Across the Deployment Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10550)
*Tejinder Singh,John Pflueger,Jeebak Mitra,Robert Lincourt,Mitchell Markow,Bhavesh A. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对推理优化进行跨部署栈的统一分析，提出三层分类法、多目标优化建模、部署排序泛函及证据协议，综合边缘平台、数据中心GPU与量化研究证据，指出部署结果由单层分析无法预测的跨层交互主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI部署性能并非仅由模型架构决定，而是受压缩、编译器变换与服务策略之间交互影响。已发表基准常在不可比条件下报告延迟与吞吐，削弱了其作为部署决策依据的价值，因此亟需统一分析框架与可比性协议。

Method: 提出三层分类法：模型级技术（量化、剪枝、蒸馏）、编译器变换（图融合、布局优化、内核自动调优）、系统策略（动态批处理、准入控制、内存分层）。将部署形式化为在精度、延迟、吞吐、内存占用和能耗上的约束多目标优化问题，分析具有帕累托单调性与尺度不变性的部署排序泛函；用Roofline模型刻画内存带宽层级对不同精度域的性能约束，用排队模型解释负载下服务时间变化对响应时间的放大。并提出证据协议，区分实测、推导与分析性结论，限定数值比较仅在同论文内进行，且要求报告硬件、软件版本、批处理语义与热状态。

Result: 综合了边缘平台（含Jetson AGX Orin及五种推理框架）、数据中心GPU（含A100、H100及三种LLM服务引擎）以及Llama-3.1系列量化研究的证据，表明部署结果由跨层交互主导，任何单层分析均无法预测。

Conclusion: 给出约束感知的选型流程，并提出编译器—服务协同优化、跨硬件性能预测与标准化能耗报告等开放问题。

Abstract: AI deployment performance is shaped not by model architecture alone, but by interactions among compression, compiler transformations, and serving policies. Published benchmarks often report latency and throughput under incomparable conditions, limiting their use for deployment decisions. This paper presents a unified analytical treatment of inference optimization across the deployment stack. We introduce a three-layer taxonomy covering model-level techniques such as quantization, pruning, and distillation; compiler transformations such as graph fusion, layout optimization, and kernel autotuning; and system policies such as dynamic batching, admission control, and memory tiering. We formulate deployment as a constrained multi-objective optimization problem over accuracy, latency, throughput, memory footprint, and energy, and analyze a deployment-ranking functional with Pareto monotonicity and scale invariance. Roofline models show how memory-bandwidth hierarchies bound performance across precision regimes, while queuing models explain how service-time changes amplify response time under load. To improve comparability, we propose an evidence protocol that separates measured, derived, and analytical claims; limits numerical comparison to within-paper results; and requires reporting of hardware, software versions, batch semantics, and thermal state. We synthesize evidence from edge platforms, including Jetson AGX Orin and five inference frameworks; data center GPUs, including A100 and H100 with three LLM serving engines; and quantization studies across the Llama-3.1 family. The synthesis shows that deployment outcomes are governed by cross-layer interactions that no single-layer analysis can predict. We conclude with a constraint-aware selection procedure and open problems in compiler-serving co-optimization, cross-hardware performance prediction, and standardized energy reporting.

</details>


### [81] [ReqEvolve: User-Oriented Software Self-Evolution through Automatic Requirement Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10590)
*Md Asif Iqbal Fahim,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReqEvolve——一个运行时代码生成系统，通过接受高层用户请求实现用户驱动的软件自演化；系统融合自动需求工程（RE）与测试驱动开发（TDD），在 72 个演化案例上取得 89.2% Pass@1，显著优于两个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件自演化范式可让系统在运行中自主扩展与重构能力，但新功能请求通常来自终端用户，且很少以技术语言表达；开发者必须先将其转译为技术规格，系统才能演化，导致用户无法立即观察目标行为、需求早期验证被延迟。

Method: ReqEvolve 是一个运行时代码生成系统，接受高层用户请求以支撑用户驱动的自演化。它集成自动需求工程（RE）与测试驱动开发（TDD），将用户请求转化为可执行功能，流程包括：需求澄清（clarification）、规格分解（specification decomposition）、测试生成（test generation）与运行时集成（runtime integration）。

Result: 在 18 个项目、72 个软件演化案例上，对照 SpecFix（面向 RE 的代码生成方法）与本系统的消融变体进行评估。ReqEvolve 达到 89.2% Pass@1，较 SpecFix 提升 18.8%（p < 0.01，r = 0.79，大效应量），较消融基线提升 32.6%（p < 0.001，r = 0.88，大效应量）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，用户驱动的自演化是一种可行范式，能够基于用户请求自主扩展软件能力，从而在开发者验证之前加速需求验证；不过该结论仅为初步证据。

Abstract: The paradigm of software self-evolution enables systems to autonomously extend and reconfigure their own capabilities during execution in response to technical specifications. Yet requests for new functionality often originate from end users and are rarely expressed in technical terms. As a result, developers must translate user needs into technical specifications before the system can evolve, delaying early validation of the requested functionality by preventing users from immediately observing the resulting behaviour. To address this gap, we present ReqEvolve, a runtime code generation system that enables user-driven self-evolution by accepting high-level user requests. The system integrates automatic requirements engineering (RE) and test-driven development (TDD) to transform these requests into executable functionality through clarification, specification decomposition, test generation, and runtime integration. We evaluate ReqEvolve on 72 software evolution cases across 18 projects against two baselines: SpecFix, an RE-focused code generation approach, and an ablation variant of our system. ReqEvolve achieves 89.2% Pass@1, outperforming SpecFix by 18.8% (p < 0.01, r = 0.79, large effect) and the ablation baseline by 32.6% (p < 0.001, r = 0.88, large effect). These results provide initial evidence that user-driven self-evolution is a viable paradigm for autonomously extending software capabilities from user requests, thereby accelerating requirements validation prior to developer verification.

</details>


### [82] [Generative AI for trustworthy systems - Towards a health check model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10595)
*Jan Bosch,Rick Kazman,Henry Muccini,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出"可信自主性健康检查模型"（Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model），一个多维度诊断工具，用于刻画组织在生成式AI辅助软件工程中如何建立信任，替代传统的单维成熟度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件密集型系统中快速应用，但现有分析工具（主要是单维成熟度模型）将重要的构型差异压缩为单一渐进轴，无法捕捉组织中信任建立方式的多样性与配置权衡。

Method: 基于对电信、汽车、国防、航空、银行、能源、政府和软件服务等领域18位资深从业者的归纳式访谈研究，构建模型：8个经验基础维度分为系统层（代理权限范围、保障机制、数据可信性、架构隔离、可追溯性与可理解性）和组织层（治理、人类监督姿态、劳动力能力可持续性），每个维度采用五级序数刻度；并叠加四种信任范式（操作性、工程性、统计性、基于隔离）作为横切覆盖。

Result: 模型为诊断性而非规范性：支持跨组织比较、揭示构型权衡、将组织定位于共享空间，而不强加单一演进路径；高层级并非必然更优，目标是各维度与组织领域及所选信任范式相匹配。

Conclusion: 提出"对齐假说"（alignment hypothesis）：有效可信性受限于代理权限范围与支撑该范围的各维度之间的契合度，错配会产生病态风险或不必要的摩擦；讨论了实践者应用方式并展望实证验证方向。

Abstract: The adoption of generative AI in software-intensive systems is proceeding rapidly, but current analytical instruments - principally unidimensional maturity models - compress important configurational variation into a single progressive axis. Drawing on an inductive interview study of eighteen senior practitioners across telecommunications, automotive, defence, aviation, banking, energy, government, and enterprise software services, this paper presents the Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model: a structured, multidimensional instrument for characterizing how organizations establish trust in GenAI-assisted software engineering. The model organizes eight empirically grounded dimensions into a system layer (Scope of Agent Authority, Assurance Mechanisms, Data Trustworthiness, Architectural Containment, Traceability & Comprehensibility) and an organizational layer (Governance, Human Oversight Posture, Workforce Capability Sustainability), each on a five-level ordinal scale. A cross-cutting overlay of four trust paradigms - operational, engineering, statistical, and containment-based - captures how trust is established, complementing the dimensions capturing what must be trustworthy. The model is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it supports cross-organizational comparison, surfaces configurational trade-offs, and locates an organization in a shared space without imposing a single progression path. Higher levels are not inherently better; the goal is alignment across dimensions appropriate to the organization's domain and chosen trust paradigm. This is formalized as the alignment hypothesis: effective trustworthiness is constrained by the fit between the scope of agent authority and the dimensions enabling it, with misalignment producing pathological risk or unnecessary friction. We discuss how practitioners can apply the model and outline directions for empirical validation.

</details>


### [83] [On the Relation between Code Quality and Machine Learning Performance: A Large-scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10610)
*Marius Mignard,Steven Costiou,Anne Etien*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对265,363个Kaggle竞赛笔记本进行大规模实证分析，发现通用Python代码质量与ML性能基本无关，ML特定代码违规与性能呈小幅负相关；流行度不指示质量或性能，作者专业度影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML社区普遍将模型性能视为首要指标，而将代码质量视为次要问题。这种取舍建立在一个几乎未被验证的假设之上：代码质量与ML性能无关。此外，实践者常复用通过社交信号（流行度、作者专业度）筛选出的笔记本代码，但这些信号作为质量代理的可靠性从未被评估。

Method: 对提交至Kaggle竞赛的265,363个Python笔记本进行大规模实证研究。采用两个静态分析工具评估代码质量：Pylint（捕捉通用Python代码质量）与SonarQube（配置34条针对数据科学和ML特定实践的规则）。

Result: 代码质量与性能的关系取决于所考量的质量维度。通用Python代码质量与ML性能脱钩，在所有观察中相关性可忽略或不显著；相比之下，ML特定违规与性能呈现一致的小幅负相关，并在所有观察中持续存在。笔记本流行度不能提供关于代码质量或性能的信息。代码专业度对质量或性能无信息价值，但竞赛专业度与更好的性能、更少的ML特定违规以及略多的Python错误和重构违规相关。

Conclusion: 代码质量与ML性能的关系并非单一维度：通用Python质量与性能解耦，而ML特定实践的质量与性能存在一致的小幅关联。社交信号作为质量代理的可靠性有限——流行度无效，作者专业度仅在竞赛经验方面对性能有一定指示作用。研究结果挑战了ML社区将代码质量视为次要问题的默认假设，并揭示了流行度和作者专业度作为质量代理的局限性。

Abstract: Context: Computational notebooks are the standard environment for machine learning (ML) development. Within the ML community, model performance is often the primary considered metric, and code quality is treated as a secondary concern. This prioritization relies on a largely untested assumption that code quality and ML performance are unrelated. Practitioners also reuse existing code that may come from notebooks selected through social signals (popularity, author expertise) whose reliability as quality proxies has never been assessed. Objective: We empirically investigated the relationship between code quality and ML performance in notebooks, and evaluated whether popularity and author expertise give indication on code quality or performance. Method: We conducted a large-scale empirical study of 265,363 Python notebooks submitted to Kaggle competitions. We assessed code quality with two static analysis tools: Pylint, capturing general Python code quality, and SonarQube, configured with a profile of 34 rules targeting data-science and ML-specific practices. Results: The relationship between code quality and performance depends on the notion of quality considered. General Python code quality is decoupled from ML performance, showing negligible or non-significant correlations across all observations. In contrast, ML-specific violations exhibit a consistent, small negative association with performance that persists across all observations. The popularity of a notebook does not give information on the code quality or performance. Code expertise provides no information on quality or performance, but competition expertise correlates with better performance, fewer ML-specific violations, and slightly more Python errors and refactoring violations.

</details>


### [84] [Numbat: Building and Verifying a Self-Contained Machine-Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10632)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文构建并验证了 numbat——一个用 Zig 单一通用语言编写、无第三方运行时依赖的机器学习栈，并通过五级差分验证协议对其正确性进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习系统几乎都依赖少数几个以 Python 编排的大型框架，继承了诸多工程负担：数百个版本耦合包构成的环境、独立的部署导出工具链、研究语言与产品部署语言的割裂。此外，验证这类栈本身比构建更难——有缺陷的训练往往不会直接报错，而是静默收敛到略差的模型。

Method: numbat 覆盖张量计算、自动微分、神经网络模块、混合精度、多 GPU 训练、数据加载与监控；SDK 通过稳定的、增量版本化的 C ABI（超 1400 个入口点）暴露，并提供六种语言绑定；临床领域平面将监管要求编码为可执行验收门。验证方面，将广泛使用的参考实现作为可执行规范，在五个层级进行验证（从算子梯度检查到针对同机参考运行的自动化轨迹门），即"轨迹级差分预言机"。

Result: 该协议发现了十处静默配方分歧，并记录了其机制与症状。作为验收测试，在 COCO 2017 上从随机初始化开始，按完整 500 epoch 计划训练了一个 25.9M 参数、YOLOv8m 级别的检测器：导出权重在官方协议下得 0.4956 mAP50-95，由参考栈自带的验证器评分（已发布端点为 0.502），单 GPU 步时间在相同硬件上持平。权重、每轮指标与完整运行清单均已发布。

Conclusion: numbat 证明了无需第三方运行时依赖、以单一通用语言构建完整机器学习栈的可行性与可验证性，其轨迹级差分验证协议能有效捕捉静默训练偏差，并在大规模检测任务上达到与参考实现相当的精度与性能。

Abstract: Machine-learning systems are built almost exclusively on a few large Python-orchestrated frameworks, and they inherit those stacks' engineering costs: environments of hundreds of version-coupled packages, separate export toolchains for deployment, and the split between the language research is written in and the language products ship in. We report on the construction and verification of numbat, a machine-learning stack written in one general-purpose language (Zig) with no third-party runtime dependencies. The stack spans tensor computation, automatic differentiation, neural-network modules, mixed precision, multi-GPU training, data loading and monitoring; an SDK exposes it behind a stable, additively versioned C ABI of over 1,400 entry points, with bindings for six languages; and its clinical domain planes encode regulatory requirements as executable acceptance gates rather than documentation. Verifying such a stack is the harder half of building it: a defective training run rarely fails, it converges quietly to a slightly worse model. We treat a widely used reference implementation as an executable specification and verify against it at five levels, from operator gradient checks to an automated trajectory gate against a same-machine reference run - the arrangement our companion study formalizes as a trajectory-level differential oracle. The protocol surfaced ten silent recipe divergences, which we catalog with mechanisms and symptoms. As the acceptance test, we train a 25.9M-parameter detector of the YOLOv8m class from random initialization on COCO 2017 for the full 500-epoch schedule: the exported weights score 0.4956 mAP50-95 under the official protocol, scored by the reference stack's own validator (published endpoint 0.502), with single-GPU step time at parity on identical hardware. Weights, per-epoch metrics and the full run manifest are released.

</details>


### [85] [Governed Human-AI Prioritization Under Uncertainty: Adaptive Estimation and Dependency-Constrained Portfolio Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10648)
*Azzeddine Ihsine,Sara Ihsine*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控合成实验，量化评估D-POAF决策实践中的五个算子（BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP），系统刻画其在排序稳定性、不确定性聚合、漂移适应、高误差检测及依赖约束组合优化中的行为，为证据加权的人机协同优先级决策提供定量基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生软件工程中，人类判断、历史类比、参数估计与AI生成预测被混合进同一优先级决策，问题不再是单纯排序，而是如何在可审查、可重校准的前提下治理异质估计、不确定性、战略参数、依赖关系与有限容量。

Method: 采用受控合成实验，设定已知潜在变量与显式误差过程，对BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP五个算子进行行为表征；通过权重扰动、可靠性加权聚合、估计量漂移、模型集体分歧检测，以及800个依赖约束组合实例的bootstrap区间分析进行评估。

Result: 中等BVS权重扰动下全局排序稳定（Spearman中位数0.986），但更大战略变动使前10%重叠降至0.788；可靠性加权工作量聚合MAE为0.616，较最佳单一估计量（1.073）降低42.6%，并优于简单均值（0.638）；漂移下自适应可靠性加权使平均RMSE相对等权降低4.5%；模型集体分歧检测最高误差优先级估计的ROC-AUC为0.906；800个依赖约束实例中，价值-工作量法相对精确最优的目标比均值为0.962、ODP距离0.953。

Conclusion: 研究为证据加权的人机AI优先级决策建立了定量基础，并界定了最短路径ODP形式与一般发布组合选择之间的优化边界；价值-工作量法在既定目标下表现出虽小但系统性的优势。

Abstract: AI-native software engineering increasingly combines human judgment, historical analogy, parametric estimation, and AI-generated forecasts inside the same prioritization decision. The resulting problem is not merely how to rank candidate work, but how to govern heterogeneous estimates, uncertainty, strategic parameters, dependencies, and limited capacity in a way that remains inspectable and recalibratable. We study five quantitative operators used in the D-POAF decision practice: Business Value Score (BVS), Effort and Risk Score (ERS), Prioritization Value Score (PVS), Collective Calibration Score (CCS), and Optimal Development Path (ODP). Controlled synthetic experiments characterize their behavior under known latent variables and explicit error processes. Moderate BVS-weight perturbations preserved global rankings (median Spearman 0.986), while broader strategic changes reduced top-10% overlap to 0.788. Reliability-weighted effort aggregation achieved MAE 0.616, a 42.6% reduction relative to the best individual estimator (MAE 1.073), and outperformed a simple mean (MAE 0.638). Under estimator drift, adaptive reliability weighting reduced average RMSE by 4.5% relative to equal weighting. Model-collective divergence detected the highest-error priority estimates with ROC-AUC 0.906. In 800 dependency-constrained portfolio instances, value-to-effort achieved mean objective ratio 0.962 and ODP distance 0.953 against the exact optimum; bootstrap intervals confirm a small but systematic advantage for value-to-effort under the declared objective. These results establish a quantitative basis for evidence-weighted human-AI prioritization and define the optimization boundary between shortest-path ODP formulations and general release-portfolio selection.

</details>


### [86] [LLMVul: A Vulnerability-Labeled Dataset of LLM-Generated C/C++ Functions from Real Production Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10945)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMVul：从真实生产仓库中挖掘的LLM生成C/C++函数漏洞标注数据集，含21,430个函数，其中1,540个被判为漏洞函数，覆盖17类CWE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据集多聚焦人工编写代码或受控提示环境，难以研究真实软件项目中LLM生成代码的安全弱点。论文旨在填补这一空白，构建源自真实生产仓库的LLM生成代码漏洞数据集。

Method: 从GitHub挖掘2022年11月13日至2026年9月3日共4年的AI辅助开发活动，利用提交元数据与AI署名证据等溯源信号；过滤与去重后得到226个仓库的21,430个唯一C/C++函数，附仓库、提交、函数、溯源及AI工具元数据；采用静态分析与模式匹配的集成方法生成漏洞标签并分配CWE类别，另做独立人工标注并以Cohen's kappa（k=0.79）评估一致性。

Result: 数据集包含1,540个集成判定为漏洞的函数，涵盖17个CWE类别，在真实LLM生成易漏洞C/C++函数数量上显著超过现有面向漏洞的LLM代码基准；人工标注一致性k=0.79，表明标签可靠。

Conclusion: LLMVul同时保留代码级漏洞标签与生成/溯源元数据，支持漏洞检测、LLM生成代码安全评估及AI辅助开发中漏洞模式分析的可复现研究，数据集已在Zenodo公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate and assist with software development, yet existing vulnerability datasets largely focus on human-written code or controlled prompting environments. This limits the ability to study security weaknesses in LLM-generated code as it appears in real-world software projects. We present LLMVul, a vulnerability-labeled dataset of LLM-generated C/C++ functions mined from real production repositories. We mine AI-assisted development activity from GitHub over a 4 year period, from November 13, 2022 to September 3, 2026, using provenance signals such as commit metadata and AI-related authorship evidence. After filtering and deduplication, LLMVul contains 21,430 unique C/C++ functions from 226 repositories, together with repository, commit, function, provenance, and AI-tool metadata. We establish vulnerability labels using an ensemble of complementary static-analysis and pattern-based techniques and assign Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to confirmed vulnerable functions. To assess labeling reliability, we additionally conduct independent manual annotation and measure inter-rater agreement using Cohen's kappa ($k=0.79$). LLMVul contains 1,540 ensemble-vulnerable functions spanning 17 unique CWE categories, providing substantially more real-world LLM-generated vulnerable C/C++ functions than existing vulnerability-oriented LLM code benchmarks. By preserving both code-level vulnerability labels and generation/provenance metadata, LLMVul enables reproducible research on vulnerability detection, security evaluation of LLM-generated code, and analysis of vulnerability patterns in AI-assisted software development. The LLMVul dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22668216.

</details>


### [87] [What a Random Draw from the MCP Registry Contains, and What Tool-Use Benchmarks Contain Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10962)
*Haseeb Mohammed Afsar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过对24,135个MCP服务器注册表进行概率抽样（400个npm/stdio服务器）并在线上实测，揭示了既有研究因抽样偏差（参考集、流行榜单、人工筛选、修复管道）而高估生态质量的问题：仅48.8%能完成初始化握手，主要失败原因是服务器根本无法启动（37.5%）而非缺少凭证（13.3%）；能运行的195个服务器在硬性规范上完全合规（2,766个工具零致命JSON Schema违规），但可选安全注解缺失率达58.8%。作者还发现真实MCP工具描述近乎无跨作者重复（0.0%），而BFCL v4存在16.7%近重复（16.4个百分点为独立任务间），且原始数据中大量为名称+描述完全重复，需全局去重后再统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP服务器生态研究在抽样时悄然偏向"能正常工作的服务器"：使用参考集、流行度列表、人工筛选框架，或采用"修复服务器直到其启动"的管道。这种选择性抽样使生态质量被系统性高估，缺少对未修复概率样本真实构成的报告。作者希望揭示未修复概率样本的真实面貌，并校正被抽样偏差扭曲的统计指标（如握手成功率、注解缺失率、工具描述重复率）。

Method: 1) 抽样：从24,135个MCP服务器注册表普查中，按公开随机种子抽取400个npm/stdio服务器。2) 实测探测：对每个服务器进行线上（over the wire）探测，检验initialize握手与JSON Schema合规性。3) 对照：以相同测量工具对比一个人工筛选框架的结果。4) 描述文本分析：用统一方法（cosine相似度）比较服务器广告的工具描述与两个工具使用基准语料（BFCL v4、UltraTool），在多阈值下测量近重复率与跨作者重复率。5) 可复现性：全部数据由公开脚本与发布种子重新生成。

Result: - 握手成功率：概率样本仅48.8%完成initialize握手，人工筛选框架为66.7%。最主要失败是服务器根本无法启动（37.5%），而非缺少凭证（13.3%）。- 硬性合规：在195个可运行服务器中，2,766个广告工具零致命JSON Schema违规，硬性规范合规率100%。- 可选安全注解：随机抽样工具级缺失率58.8%，人工筛选框架为41.5%，说明筛选同样美化该指标。- 工具描述重复：真实MCP工具在cosine 0.70下近重复率仅2.8%，且全部发生在单个服务器内部，跨作者近重复在所有测试阈值下均为0.0%；BFCL v4近重复率16.7%（其中16.4个百分点位于独立呈现的任务之间）；UltraTool仅0.3%，比真实工具更干净，说明重复是BFCL的特性而非合成语料的共性。- 数据冗余：原始BFCL行中68.8%、UltraTool行中85.6%为名称+描述完全重复，而真实MCP仅0.4%；若不全局去重，基于这些发布的统计测量的是重复而非工具本身。

Conclusion: 未修复的概率样本揭示了MCP生态中被抽样偏差掩盖的真实状况：近半服务器无法完成握手，主因是无法启动；能运行的服务器硬性规范合规，差异集中在可选注解。同时，基准语料（尤其BFCL）存在严重的近重复与完全重复，需全局去重后统计才有意义。研究强调概率抽样与可复现（公开脚本+发布种子）对生态测量的必要性，并提示既有依赖参考集或修复管道的结论可能被系统性高估。所有数据可由发布脚本与公开种子重新生成。

Abstract: Studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server ecosystem draw their samples in ways that quietly select for servers that work: reference sets, popularity lists, hand-curated frames, or pipelines that repair a server until it starts. We report what an unrepaired probability sample actually contains. From a 24,135-server registry census we draw 400 npm/stdio servers with a published seed and probe each one over the wire. Only 48.8% complete an initialize handshake, against 66.7% for a hand-curated frame measured with the same instrument, and the dominant failure is not missing credentials (13.3%) but servers that never start at all (37.5%). Among the 195 that do run, hard conformance is total: zero fatal JSON Schema violations across 2,766 advertised tools. Optional safety annotations are the real variance, and the tool-level omission rate on a random draw is 58.8% against 41.5% on the curated frame, so curation flatters this figure too. We then compare the tool descriptions these servers advertise against two tool-use benchmark corpora using one method held constant. Real MCP tools show 2.8% near-duplication at cosine 0.70, and all of it lies within single servers: cross-author near-duplication is 0.0% at every threshold tested. BFCL v4 shows 16.7%, of which 16.4 points lie between independently presented tasks. UltraTool shows 0.3%, cleaner than real tools, so this is a property of BFCL and not of synthetic corpora as a class. Separately, 68.8% of raw BFCL rows and 85.6% of raw UltraTool rows are exact name-plus-description repeats, against 0.4% for real MCP, so any statistic computed over these releases without global deduplication measures repetition rather than tools. All figures regenerate from released scripts and a published seed.

</details>


### [88] [Engineering Reliable Commit Gates for Agentic AI: Cost-Aware Verification Portfolios under Common-Mode Data Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10969)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 VP-CONTROL——面向智能体状态变更动作的成本感知提交门（commit gate）运行时保障设计与确定性基准，通过48个任务模板生成2880个场景、六类故障机制，分离验证器模型多样性与证据源多样性，并验证提交时强制执行的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统会执行改变状态的动作，而附加验证器本身可能继承同一上游故障，导致不安全的写入被批准；现有验证策略缺乏对证据来源独立性、验证成本与提交时执行强制的系统性评估。

Method: 设计 VP-CONTROL 确定性基准：48个任务模板×六类故障机制=2880场景；采用固定调用次数的2×2实验分离验证器模型多样性与证据源多样性；使用组合控制器仅基于部署可观测元数据选择验证计划；近似簇调整校准设定名义5%每任务目标；预注册在线 HTTP/SQLite 研究测试并发写入与响应丢失，对比仅验证器门、事务性部分守卫与完全原子守卫，以及幂等请求标识。

Result: 跨模型投票在共享证据上批准62.9%不安全提案，而独立来源仅22.9%，来源效应40.9个百分点，模型多样性效应11.3个百分点；锁定测试上近似校准实现1.9%不安全执行与38.2%自动化安全覆盖；匹配预算组合优于固定验证策略；未见故障族风险16-26%，FinQA检验未能复现来源效应；在线研究中仅验证器门被后检竞争击败，完全原子守卫在216个回合中无不安全效果，幂等标识防止响应丢失后的重复效果。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式证据谱系、成本感知选择与提交时强制执行，同时暴露近似校准与本地工具泛化的局限。

Abstract: Agentic systems commit state-changing actions, but additional verifiers can inherit the same upstream fault. We present VP-CONTROL, a runtime-assurance design and deterministic benchmark for cost-aware commit gates. Its 48 task templates yield 2,880 scenarios across six fault regimes. A fixed-call 2 x 2 experiment separates verifier-model diversity from evidence-source diversity. On frozen proposals from two local actor families, a cross-model vote over shared evidence approves 62.9% of unsafe proposals, versus 22.9% with an independent source. The source effect is 40.9 percentage points, compared with 11.3 for model diversity. A portfolio controller selects verification plans using only deployment-observable metadata. Approximate cluster-adjusted calibration at a nominal 5% per-task target yields 1.9% unsafe execution and 38.2% automated safe coverage on the locked test. Matched-budget portfolios also improve on fixed verification policies. Transfer remains conditional: unseen fault families yield 16-26% risk, and a FinQA check fails to reproduce the source effect with the tested small verifiers. A preregistered live HTTP/SQLite study tests concurrent writes and lost responses. After-check races defeat verifier-only gates; transactional partial guards prevent only covered failures, while a full atomic guard records no unsafe effects across 216 episodes. Idempotent request identifiers prevent duplicate effects after lost responses. The results motivate explicit evidence lineage, cost-aware selection, and commit-time enforcement, while exposing the limits of approximate calibration and local-tool generalization.

</details>


### [89] [FST Pay: Deterministic Safety-Gated Architecture for Youth Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11195)
*Shaikh Mohammed Burhan,Syed Farhaan Quadri,Tabassum Nahid Sultana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FST Pay 提出一种面向青少年实时支付的架构与形式化规范，将实时授权路径与生成式 AI 严格解耦：授权路径仅执行六个确定性不变量检查并输出 ALLOW/REVIEW/BLOCK，生成式 AI 仅在下游消费事后事件生成解释，无账本变更权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字支付基础设施使青少年直接接入实时金融服务，虽促进金融素养与数字普惠，但带来冲动消费、社会工程欺诈、未授权交易与商户剥削等风险。传统对策依赖概率性机器学习或刚性静态控制；而让概率性/生成式 AI 直接影响实时支付授权会引入非确定性、不可预测的边界行为与审计漏洞。因此需要一个确定性的安全边界。

Method: 提出 FST Pay 架构与形式化规范。核心是不可变操作边界：实时授权路径上采用严格确定性安全门控，下游 AI 解释与之解耦。对经 UPI 等通道发起的交易执行六项确定性不变量检查——消费限额、监护人共签策略、交易金额阈值、商户类别码（MCC）、时间访问区间、硬件完整性约束。通过有序且互斥的决策函数将交易严格分类为 ALLOW、REVIEW 或 BLOCK。高风险交易触发异步监护人共签工作流。生成式 AI 完全置于结算下游，仅消费已发布的事后决策事件以生成自然语言财务洞察，不具备对账本的变更权限。

Result: 论文为架构与形式化规范类工作，未报告实证或量化实验结果；其主张的产出是：实时授权路径的确定性保证、互斥三态决策的规范定义，以及生成式 AI 在结算下游的无变更权限定位，从而规避实时授权中的非确定性与审计漏洞。

Conclusion: 在青少年实时支付场景中，应坚持“实时授权确定性、AI 解释下游化”的原则：以确定性不变量门控与异步监护人共签保障安全与可审计性，同时保留生成式 AI 在金融洞察方面的价值，而不让其介入授权决策。

Abstract: Digital payment infrastructures increasingly provide adolescent users with direct access to real-time financial services. While early access promotes financial literacy and digital inclusion, it exposes young users to severe risks of impulsive spending, social engineering frauds, unauthorized transactions, and merchant exploitation. Conventional countermeasures rely on probabilistic machine learning or rigid static controls. However, allowing probabilistic or generative artificial intelligence (AI) models to directly influence real-time payment authorization introduces non-determinism, unpredictable edge-case behavior, and critical audit vulnerabilities. This paper introduces Financial Safety for Teens Pay (FST Pay) as an architectural and formal specification. FST Pay is founded on an immutable operational boundary: strict deterministic safety gating on the real-time authorization path coupled with decoupled downstream AI explanation. Transactions initiated via rails like UPI are subjected to six deterministic invariant checks covering spending limits, guardian co-sign policies, transaction amount thresholds, merchant category codes, temporal access intervals, and hardware integrity constraints. Transactions are classified strictly into ALLOW, REVIEW, or BLOCK outcomes through an ordered, mutually exclusive decision function. High-risk transactions trigger an asynchronous guardian co-sign workflow. Generative AI is relegated entirely downstream of settlement, consuming published post-decision events solely to generate natural-language financial insights without holding mutation privileges over the ledger.

</details>


### [90] [TripleBound: Triplet-Guided Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Microservice Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11212)
*Mineth Weerasinghe,Himindu Kularathne,Methmini Madhushika,Danuka Lakshan,Nisansa de Silva,Adeesha Wijayasiri,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TripleBound，一种结合异构图表征与弱监督三元组约束的混合框架，用于单体到微服务的自动拆分，并在多个基准上取得较优的复合分解得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体向微服务迁移面临紧耦合与服务边界不清晰的问题；现有拆分方法通常仅依赖结构依赖或语义相似度单一信号，很少在统一表征学习目标中融合二者。

Method: 以 CHGNN 将单体建模为含程序节点、资源节点、CALL 边与 CRUD 边的异构图以捕获结构依赖；同时基于包结构、命名约定与代码位置，由解析器推断服务组以生成弱监督三元组约束，并将该约束直接注入共享的结构隐空间，使结构与语义信号在表征学习中联合优化。

Result: 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、PlantsByWebSphere、JPetStore 上评估：在所选权重下，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、JPetStore 上取得最高复合分解得分，优于 CHGNN 与 MonoEmbed；在 PlantsByWebSphere 上 CHGNN 更强。分指标分析显示结构模块性与分区间耦合改善的同时，部分数据集上实体分布不平衡加剧。更换复合权重后，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir 与 DayTrader 保持第一，但 JPetStore 上不再保持第一，表明总体排名依赖指标权重。

Conclusion: 结构与语义信号在共享隐空间中的联合优化可提升单体拆分整体效果，但收益存在指标间权衡，且综合排名对评价权重敏感，说明此类方法的优势并非在所有数据集与聚合方式下都稳健。

Abstract: Cloud computing and DevOps have made microservices a common architecture for scalable, maintainable software systems. However, migrating monoliths to microservices remains challenging due to tight coupling and unclear service boundaries. Existing decomposition approaches typically rely on either structural dependencies or semantic similarity signals, but rarely integrate both within a unified representation learning objective. This paper proposes TripleBound, a hybrid framework for automated monolith-to-microservices decomposition that augments a heterogeneous graph neural network with weakly supervised triplet constraints derived from parser-inferred service groups based on package structure, naming conventions, and code location. TripleBound injects triplet-based constraints directly into the shared structural latent space, enabling both signals to be jointly optimized during representation learning. Structural dependencies are captured using CHGNN, which models the monolith as a heterogeneous graph with program nodes, resource nodes, CALL edges, and CRUD edges. Semantic relationships are incorporated through triplet constraints generated from parser-inferred service groups. Evaluation on AcmeAir, DayTrader, PlantsByWebSphere, and JPetStore shows that TripleBound achieves the highest composite decomposition score under the selected weighting on AcmeAir, DayTrader, and JPetStore compared to CHGNN and MonoEmbed, while CHGNN remains stronger on PlantsByWebSphere. Per-metric analysis reveals trade-offs: gains in structural modularity and inter-partition coupling are accompanied by higher entity distribution imbalance on some datasets. Alternative composite weightings preserve TripleBound's first-place ranking on AcmeAir and DayTrader but not on JPetStore, showing that the aggregate ranking is metric-dependent.

</details>


### [91] [Exploring the Role of Security Experience and ChatGPT Usage Strategies on Secure Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11303)
*Alessio Ferrari,Minh An Nguyen,Kushal Ramkumar,Liliana Pasquale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项针对26名网络安全硕士生的探索性研究分析了其修复漏洞作业中的ChatGPT交互日志：不同网络安全专业水平的学生使用策略大体相似，单一使用模式与成绩的关联在多重重比较校正后不显著，而使用模式的多样性（采用不同使用模式的数量）即使在控制专业知识后仍与成绩正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程教育中被快速采纳，但其在"安全软件工程教育"中的角色研究不足，尤其缺乏对学生实际如何使用ChatGPT及其与学习成效关系的实证证据，因此需要探索性研究来刻画使用行为并识别与绩效相关的因素。

Method: 对某兼职网络安全硕士项目中26名研究生在漏洞修复作业期间使用ChatGPT的情况开展探索性实证研究；采集学生的ChatGPT交互日志，采用结构化双重编码（double-coding）程序对使用模式进行归类，并分析使用模式与既有网络安全专业知识是否与作业成绩相关。

Result: 不同网络安全专业水平的学生使用的ChatGPT策略大体相似；按成绩分组，个体使用模式呈现描述性差异，但在多重比较校正后均不具统计显著性；相反，ChatGPT使用多样性（即采用的不同使用模式数量）与成绩呈正相关，且在控制网络安全专业知识后依然成立。

Conclusion: 学生"如何"使用ChatGPT可能比"是否"使用更具信息价值；这些探索性发现为后续受控研究提供动因，以引导学生更有效地在安全软件工程教育中使用LLM。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is reshaping software engineering education, but their role in secure software engineering education remains underexplored. We report an exploratory empirical study of how 26 graduate students in a part-time MSc Cybersecurity programme used ChatGPT during a vulnerability-fixing assignment. To characterise ChatGPT use, we analysed students' ChatGPT interaction logs using a structured double-coding procedure and examined whether usage patterns and prior cybersecurity expertise were associated with assignment performance. The results show that students with varying levels of cybersecurity expertise used broadly similar ChatGPT strategies. Individual usage patterns showed descriptive differences by grade, but none remained statistically significant after correcting for multiple comparisons. In contrast, diversity of ChatGPT usage, i.e., the number of distinct usage patterns adopted, was positively associated with performance, even after controlling for cybersecurity expertise. These exploratory findings suggest that the way students engage with ChatGPT may be more informative than whether they use it, and motivate future controlled studies to guide students toward effective LLM use in secure software engineering education.

</details>


### [92] [Agent-Integrated Software: Interaction Contracts and Continuous Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11381)
*Shengcheng Yu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将智能体嵌入既有应用会带来持续性的协调问题，需要任务级交互与应用行为之间的显式对应。本文提出AIS软件模式与IIA任务语义，用开放转移系统模型、交互契约与持续保证来支撑可维护的智能体集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在既有应用中嵌入智能体后，用户在委托执行持续进行时仍可修改目标并操作共享对象，由此产生持续的协调问题。作者认为，可靠的集成不能仅依赖局部组件的正确性，而需要在任务级交互与应用行为之间建立显式对应关系。

Method: 提出Agent-Integrated Software（AIS）软件模式，包含常规内核、直接交互与内置智能体；提出Intent-Level Interaction Abstraction（IIA）作为用户检视与控制委托工作的任务语义。用开放转移系统模型刻画AIS执行与IIA状态、事件的关系；以交互契约通过任务绑定、角色特定权限、控制转换与结果证据约束该关系；以持续保证在依赖变化时维护受限声明。并用紧凑的披露契约与条件命题说明局部组件有效性不足，以及如何将选定的准入门槛不变量与规划分离。

Result: 通过紧凑的披露契约和条件命题，论证了局部组件有效性不足以支撑可靠集成，并说明选定的准入不变量可以与规划相分离。跨软件领域的对比分析揭示了该框架的假设与适用边界。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一条研究议程，涵盖应用抽象、开发支持、受控执行、质量评估与人类监督，目标是把智能体集成发展为可维护的软件工程学科。

Abstract: Embedding an intelligent agent in an existing application creates a persistent coordination problem: users can revise goals and manipulate shared objects while delegated execution continues. We argue that dependable integration requires an explicit correspondence between task-level interaction and application behavior. We introduce Agent-Integrated Software (AIS) as a software pattern combining a conventional core, direct interaction, and a built-in agent, and Intent-Level Interaction Abstraction (IIA) as the task semantics through which users inspect and control delegated work. An open transition-system model relates AIS execution to IIA states and events. Interaction contracts constrain this relation through task bindings, role-specific authority, control transitions, and outcome evidence; continuous assurance maintains scoped claims as their dependencies change. A compact disclosure contract and conditional propositions illustrate why local component validity is insufficient and how selected admission invariants can be separated from planning. Contrasting software domains expose the framework's assumptions and limits. This perspective develops a research agenda spanning application abstraction, development support, controlled execution, quality assessment, and human supervision, with the aim of making agent integration a maintainable software engineering discipline.

</details>


### [93] [Ecdysis: Efficient and Effective Training of Runtime Harnesses for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11677)
*Ruiqing Yue,Yu Cui,Zhuoyu Sun,Sicheng Pan,Xianhong Xue,Tingyu Li,Ting Li,Wenzhuo Zhu,Yi Chen,Yifei Liu,Baohan Huang,Zhe Cui,Haibin Zhang,Cong Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ecdysis：一种通过跨实例失败聚合与失败驱动协同精炼实现高效鲁棒harness进化的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness进化方法依赖迭代搜索，反复执行与修改候选harness，导致时间开销大，且易过拟合已见任务与特定失败模式，泛化能力下降。核心瓶颈在于缺乏原则性的失败诊断：观测失败可能源于模型特有缺陷或系统性harness缺陷，直接针对个体失败优化会引入不必要的模型特化适配。

Method: Ecdysis区分“模型特化适配”与“harness层面修复”，通过识别跨任务重复失败模式，将适应偏向系统性harness缺陷。采用批级跨实例失败聚合范式，联合分析多个任务实例的失败证据；引入失败驱动协同精炼（Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement），诊断失败成因并迭代精炼harness修改规范；结合跨实例失败分析与多角色诊断。

Result: 与现有harness进化方法相比，Ecdysis在harness训练上最高实现1.84倍加速，同时使所得harness的推理准确率提升18.56%。

Conclusion: 以原则性失败诊断为突破口，Ecdysis通过跨实例失败聚合与多角色协同诊断，实现更高效、更有效的harness进化，兼顾训练效率与泛化性能。

Abstract: Self-evolving runtime harnesses can substantially improve the capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents and provide a promising paradigm for optimizing agent execution. Existing harness evolution methods typically rely on iterative search, repeatedly evaluating and revising candidate harnesses based on execution feedback from task instances. While this paradigm enables continuous harness optimization, it incurs substantial time overhead due to repeated agent executions and code modifications, and may overfit to observed tasks and specific failure patterns, resulting in degraded generalization to unseen tasks. We identify the lack of principled failure diagnosis as a key bottleneck in harness evolution: an observed failure can reflect either model-specific deficiencies or systematic harness deficiencies, and directly optimizing against individual failures can lead to unnecessary model-specific accommodation. We therefore propose Ecdysis, an efficient and effective framework that distinguishes model-specific accommodation from harness-level repair and biases adaptation toward systematic harness deficiencies by identifying recurring cross-task failure patterns. Ecdysis adopts a batch-level cross-instance failure aggregation paradigm to jointly analyze failure evidence from multiple task instances and further introduces Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement to diagnose failure causes and iteratively refine harness modification specifications. By combining cross-instance failure analysis with multi-role diagnosis, Ecdysis enables more effective harness evolution with lower training time. Experiments show that Ecdysis achieves up to a 1.84x speedup in harness training compared with existing harness evolution methods, while improving the reasoning accuracy of the resulting harnesses by 18.56%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过语义先验、双粒度MoE与转向加权损失，在丹麦AIS数据集的1-4小时预测中持续优于现有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶轨迹预测面临三大挑战：行为多模态性、语义信息利用不足，以及长期预测中的误差累积问题。

Method: 引入船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验；构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练LLM编码静态语义并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；设计双粒度MoE架构，序列级专家建模全局航线趋势，token级专家细化局部操纵行为；提出转向加权交叉熵损失以缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布。

Result: 在真实丹麦AIS数据集上，1至4小时预测区间内持续优于SOTA基线；4小时预测中ADE与FDE分别较最强基线降低4.4%和5.1%；定性与消融分析表明语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度MoE提升复杂水道与航线分支场景的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的层次化预测范式，联合建模高层航行意图与局部运动动态，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [95] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Halo 方法：在深度预测模型中额外输出尺度参数并以负对数似然训练，可在时间序列任务中显著改善点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测（网络同时估计位置与尺度参数）通常用于不确定性量化。在时间序列以外领域，异方差估计对点估计的改进存在负面报告，本文旨在探究其在时间序列预测中是否也能提升点估计。

Method: Halo 复用现有深度预测器架构，增加一个输出以估计其隐含分布的尺度，并在匹配的负对数似然下训练。在三种先进模型（Transformer、图网络+变分自编码器、单层卷积网络）上，分别采用高斯与拉普拉斯损失进行适配验证。

Result: 在标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场上，Halo 在 30 组模型-市场-指标对比中的 28 组改善了 MSE 与 MAE，平均 MSE 降低 2.6%–16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7%–11.0%。

Conclusion: 两点发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络，影响远小于网络是否估计尺度；(2) 改进在已为点估计基线调优的超参数下依然成立，因此重新调参是可选的。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [96] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样把文本提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度耦合进密度拓扑优化循环，实现以自然语言表达设计意图，并在多域多物理问题中获得显著柔度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承力构型（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高单位质量刚度，但经典拓扑优化很少生成此类几何，且缺乏用自然语言表达结构设计意图的手段。作者希望把工程师意图转化为机器可解释的表示，并复用预训练生成模型作为免训练先验。

Method: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型视为免训练设计知识源，用分数蒸馏采样（SDS）把其知识蒸馏进基于密度的拓扑优化物理循环。每次迭代中，将提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度相结合，由物理决定哪些提示诱导特征得以保留；采用Heaviside投影配合β-continuation抑制中间密度；并构建基于骨架的自动化流程，将优化密度场转换为面向CAD的候选几何。

Result: 在覆盖四个几何域、两种物理机制的245次主SDS运行中，49个"提示-域"组合中有38个取得统计显著的柔度下降，机械问题最高达-31.5%，热弹性问题达-23.0%，优于基于梯度的基线。跨域形态分析发现反复出现的改进结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制了肋骨架中的死端分支，端点-柔度相关系数为r=+0.56至+0.99。Heaviside投影配合β-continuation将明显的中间密度倾向从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可将生成先验跨域、跨载荷工况和跨物理目标重定向（每个新问题的物理设置单独指定），该框架把预训练生成模型用作工程设计中可复用、免训练的先验，并将自然语言作为显式、机器可解释的设计意图表示。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [97] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在室内火灾检测中的应用，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端及固定聚合服务器的单点信任问题，提出数据集、可边缘部署压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒FL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测依赖边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中采集，因此适合联邦学习；但现有联邦方案存在三个实际障碍：上行带宽有限、存在拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端、以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。

Method: 工作包含三部分：(i) 从八个公开来源构建的室内火灾检测精选数据集；(ii) 可边缘部署的检测器，其模型更新可压缩至最多10倍；以及(iii) 半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，将历史感知聚合与轮换协调者相结合，以剔除单轮过滤器遗漏的隐蔽攻击，并消除固定服务器单点故障。

Result: 在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法的准确率与检测速度与固定服务器方案相当；一个物理分布的六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方案同时缓解带宽、拜占庭鲁棒性与固定服务器信任三项问题，且在准确性、检测速度与部署可行性方面均得到验证。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [98] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统检索2015年以来的96篇文献，评估AI与深度学习（尤其是CNN、迁移学习和数据增强）在肺癌影像学检测中的应用，认为其可作为肺癌早期诊断的有效手段，具有较高敏感性和特异性，但仍面临数据标准化、模型可解释性、患者隐私及伦理社会等挑战，需进一步研究与监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要死因之一，早期诊断对改善患者预后和生活质量至关重要；但医学影像解读过程复杂，依赖受训专家。因此，亟需探索AI与深度学习作为自动化、优化影像分析的工具。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出2015年至今涉及AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的96篇文章进行综述，重点关注卷积神经网络（CNN）结合迁移学习和数据增强等技术。

Result: AI与深度学习在肺癌早期诊断中可提供有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感性和特异性；CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强被认为是提升影像解读准确性和效率的有前景技术。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习可对肺癌患者诊疗产生积极影响，但当前仍存在标准化数据缺乏、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私保护以及伦理和社会影响等局限与挑战；需开展进一步研究并建立相应监管，以保证其临床应用的质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [99] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoSteer，一种基于优化的保范激活引导方法，将激活引导建模为黎曼优化问题，通过表示流形上的小测地步序列自适应更新激活，在保持激活范数的同时提升引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法存在两大局限：一是依赖预定义的引导轨迹，二是采用单步更新，难以捕捉激活分布的复杂结构，可能导致引导效果不佳。

Method: GeoSteer将激活引导形式化为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上通过一系列小测地步更新激活；并学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数以区分期望与不期望的激活，自适应地指导每一步引导，从而避免固定的引导方向，形成多步几何感知的优化框架。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts和UltraFeedback三个基准上，GeoSteer持续优于当前最先进的激活引导基线方法。

Conclusion: 用自适应的几何感知优化替代预定义的单步编辑，可使保范激活引导更加有效，同时保持激活范数、减少表示坍缩与退化风险，产生更平滑、稳定且一致的引导行为。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [100] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"保形校准迁移"问题：当标注校准数据仅存在于源空间、而预测集需在通过无标签配对观测关联的目标空间给出时，如何实现目标域覆盖保证。作者提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)，先将源空间标注校准迁移至目标空间，再仅用无标签目标输入校正残余失配，并给出自适应于可观测失配度量的有限样本目标域覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测依赖校准数据与部署数据的可交换性，但实际中该假设常不成立：标注数据仅在源空间可得，而目标空间缺少标注，仅有与源空间配对的无标签观测（如跨模态、传感器变更）。因此需要一种在校准迁移后仍能保持目标域覆盖的校准方法。

Method: 提出TCC框架：①利用配对数据将带标注的源校准集迁移到目标空间；②仅用无标签目标输入校正迁移后的残余失配。给出两种互补实现：TCC-KS 使用无标签不确定性代理量检测失配并进行保守校准调整；weighted-TCC 在权重稳定时对迁移校准进行重加权以提升效率。理论上提供适应可观测失配度量的有限样本目标域覆盖保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS上，方法在无标注目标校准数据条件下实现了可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断指标能够预测何时需要进行校正。

Conclusion: TCC在配对无标签数据场景下实现了跨空间校准迁移与覆盖保证，无需目标域标注，并通过无标签诊断指导校正策略，为不可交换条件下的保形预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [101] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出“完美混叠”现象：在“真实报告”与“规定动作”重合的语境中拟合的真值探针，无法仅从拟合标签区分二者；通过随机码本与混合语境拟合可将语义动作与规定输出符号、真值分离。Gemma-2-9B 实验显示传统探针 AUROC 仅 0.006，混合拟合探针达 1.000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针（probe）常用于检测模型内部表征中的“真值”或“欺骗”，但当训练语境的标签同时对应真值报告与任务规定动作时，探针所测语义无法被唯一识别。作者希望揭示这一可识别性失败的根源，并给出区分不同语义目标的方法。

Method: 在受控二元报告博弈中形式化“完美混叠”：合规语境下真值探针与规定动作探针求解同一优化问题；在对手语境下标签互补，导致 AUROC 之和恒为 1（在 751 组 cell-layer 对上精确成立）。方法上先用随机码本将“规定输出符号”与“语义动作”分离，再通过在合规与对手语境的混合数据上拟合，将“真值”与“规定动作”分离。以奖励训练得到的 Gemma-2-9B 策略为对象，比较传统合规拟合探针与混合拟合探针在留出激活上的表现。

Result: 对手语境下真值探针与规定动作探针的 AUC 互补关系在 751 组 cell-layer 对上达到浮点精度一致。对奖励训练、在所有评估对手试次中给出虚假回答的 Gemma-2-9B 策略，传统探针跨三个训练种子的 AUROC 为 0.006 ± 0.005，而混合拟合探针在同一留出激活上达到 1.000。两个分布内均完美的合规拟合探针，在同一对手激活上分别得 0.080 与 0.986。

Conclusion: 该发现针对“探针测量的是什么”，证明混合拟合探针所恢复的方向具有线性可分性，但并不建立模型保留了功能性信念、所恢复方向被因果使用，或存在可部署的欺骗检测器；由于混合拟合使用了更多训练样本并接触了带标签的对手语境，该比较仅确立线性可恢复性，而非隔离去相关带来的收益。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应边距序数损失（AMOL）与中心对冲率（CHR）指标，以抑制序数分类中的"中心类对冲"失效模式，并在四个基准上取得最优或并列最优的QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失会使序数分类网络将预测向中心类偏移，即"中心类对冲"：预测中间类可使期望对称损失最小，成为最省力路径，而现有序数损失未针对"真值相对中心位置"这一条件直接抑制该问题。

Method: 提出AMOL：对逐类损失项施加乘性权重 m(k,y)=1+α·(1−|k−c|/c)·(|y−c|/c)，其中c为中心类、k为候选类、y为真实标签；该权重为联合条件，仅当候选类靠近中心且真值远离中心时较大，否则退化为标准行为。同时提出CHR作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标，并提出非对称变体AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子下，AMOL相较交叉熵、OLL、SORD基线在全部四个数据集上取得最优或并列最优的二次加权Kappa（QWK）。在Abalone数据集上，AMOL-asym完全消除中心类对冲（五个种子下CHR=0.000±0.000，每次运行约n≈266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过按真值相对中心位置自适应加权，有效抑制中心类对冲并提升序数分类性能，CHR可作为量化该失效模式的有效诊断指标。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [103] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将持续强化学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡重新表述为赋权-可塑性权衡，并给出用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优方程。可塑性定义为观测到动作的广义有向信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中稳定性与可塑性的权衡是核心难题。Abel等(2025)将可塑性定义为观测到动作的广义有向信息、赋权定义为动作到观测的广义有向信息，把传统权衡改写为赋权-可塑性权衡；但赋权优化研究丰富，新定义下的可塑性优化仍属空白。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程框架下开展可塑性优化的初步研究，推导与赋权优化工作相类似的、用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优方程。

Result: 证明了在马尔可夫决策过程中存在用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优方程，其形式与既有赋权优化工作相对应。

Conclusion: 该工作为在新定义下优化可塑性提供了理论基础，属初步探索，为后续持续强化学习中权衡问题的研究奠定方向。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [104] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实边缘化（CF marginalisation）作为测试时评估方法，用于衡量分类模型对人口学/采集相关捷径变量的鲁棒性，并定义CF风险、校准、稳定性与最坏情况敏感性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能取得良好测试性能，但实际依赖人口统计学或采集相关的捷径特征，导致鲁棒性不足；需要一种能定量评估模型对此类干扰变量鲁棒性的测试时评估手段。

Method: 给定一个反事实图像生成器，对每个测试图像干预年龄、性别等干扰父变量，生成反事实版本，并在目标干预分布上对预测取平均，得到干预感知预测，从而边缘化人口学效应同时保留患者特异性潜信息；基于该预测定义CF风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感性等指标。

Result: 展示了该框架用于定量鲁棒性评估的有效性。

Conclusion: 反事实边缘化可作为评估分类模型对人口学/采集捷径变量鲁棒性的测试时评估框架，并支持多种定量指标。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [105] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 G2QDR 框架，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度，并将其转化为辅助稠密奖励，从而改进基于图的 GCHRL 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的 GCHRL 方法多将图仅视为随机采样工具，忽略了图所蕴含的内在连通性信息，无法充分利用底层拓扑进行高效学习；这一问题在准度量环境中尤为突出，因为状态转移的固有非对称性给稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划带来根本挑战。

Method: 引入状态连通性模型，用于预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度；将这些连通强度转换为标量辅助稠密奖励，在多个层级上提供连续引导；该模型通过在探索过程中生成的有向状态图上训练的神经网络高效实现，并可理论上集成到任意现有 GCHRL 架构中。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境中的实证结果表明，G2QDR 总体上能以可接受的计算开销提升基线 GCHRL 方法的性能。

Conclusion: G2QDR 通过显式建模并利用图的连通性信息，弥补了现有图基 GCHRL 方法忽视状态可达性的缺陷，为准度量环境提供了一种通用且高效的稠密奖励增强方案。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [106] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现 DR-LabStack，一个 React-Flask Web 系统，用于统一集成四个异构的糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预训练预测模型，实现跨模型的特征输入协调、后端适配与统一输出展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DR 预训练模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理要求和输出语义上各不相同，缺乏统一的临床交互接口，导致这些模型难以在共同界面下被访问和使用，亟需显式的界面与推理服务协调机制。

Method: 构建 React 前端与 Flask 后端组成的 Web 系统，集成 RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost 和 Two-level Ensemble 四个外部模型。共用表单检索有序模型特征，动态渲染数值与类别控件并构造位置输入向量；后端适配器加载异构模型工件并应用集成模型附带的 scaler；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类展示及方法与来源信息。功能评估于 2026 年 9 月 8 日在文档化的隔离环境中使用复制的应用文件和真实模型工件进行。

Result: 四个模型均成功加载并暴露 14、6、8 和 25 字段的输入契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画了服务行为，12 项受限向量检查确认了调用路径与阈值一致性，24 个带模拟传输的浏览器组件场景验证了输入排序与结果渲染并刻画了输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 该系统为异构 DR 模型提供了可复用的交互与服务化工作流；其贡献在于 Web 系统设计、集成与软件功能层面，而临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需另行评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [107] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiVaT-Fuse，将图像-元数据融合定义为样本级隐状态估计，用可靠性校准的变分张量融合替代直接聚合，在预测排名与扰动稳定性上优于表示级基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-元数据预测需融合异质证据，其可靠性随样本与潜在因素变化。现有表示级融合方法（拼接、门控、条件调制、注意力）只选择聚合架构，未明确定义模态不确定性下融合表示应具有的含义，也以标量置信度刻画模态可靠性，表达不足。

Method: 提出RiVaT-Fuse可靠性校准变分张量融合框架：不直接聚合出融合向量，而是通过变分目标估计共识隐状态，目标平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；以矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度；将交互分解为加性、乘性与关系性分量；将隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测耦合；给出隐式求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用低秩加对角信任算子高效实例化。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在所有直接表示级基线中取得最佳整体预测排名，同时在扰动下提升了概率稳定性与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 融合应被建模为样本级隐状态估计而非固定架构下的向量聚合；矩阵值信任几何与张量式交互分解为模态不确定性下的融合提供了更具解释性与鲁棒性的范式。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators.
